📖 LEARNED: Debris Shelter (Level 1). A lean-to of branches and leaves beats nothing. Dead air is insulation.
📖 LEARNED: Calming Panic (Level 1). Ground them: 5 things you see, 4 you hear, 3 you touch. Breathe with them. Slow is fast.
📖 LEARNED: Small Unit Tactics (Level 1). Flank, don't charge. High ground matters. Never split your group unless you have to.
📖 LEARNED: Improvised Weapons (Level 1). Everything is a weapon if you're desperate. A rock, a stick, a handful of dirt in the eyes.
📖 LEARNED: Managing Fear (Level 1). Fear is data, not orders. Acknowledge it, then decide anyway. Courage is fear that kept going.
📖 LEARNED: Tracking People (Level 1). People leave sign: broken twigs, footprints, disturbed earth. You can follow.
📖 LEARNED: Night Hunting (Level 1). Hunting at night is not hunting blind. It is hunting by ear, by smell, by patience.
📖 LEARNED: Shield Wall (Level 1). Shoulder to shoulder in the wilds, you brace as one. While an ally fights beside you, blows that reach you land softer — the line holds together or not at all.
📖 LEARNED: Tripwire (Level 1). You string a wire at ankle height. The next monster that moves eats dirt — prone, stunned, and embarrassed. (Sets up the party.)
